### 2026-08-20 - Signed organization sharing trust boundary implemented locally

- Context: M12 required opt-in organization sharing without turning downloaded packages, marketplace identity, or publisher metadata into ambient execution authority.
- Decision/files: Schema 34 adds durable trusted publishers and immutable organization-share reviews. Ed25519 schema-1 packages bind an exact canonical HTTPS registry, immutable publisher/key identity, package content, and domain-separated evidence hashes. Trust is opt-in; publisher revocation is terminal; current trust is reverified before import; review is distinct from a reasoned import/reject mutation. Desktop TypeScript, production build, 41/41 component tests, 11/11 Playwright tests including accessibility and signed review/import, docs validation, and diff hygiene pass.
- Disk: Native verification grew the repository to 58.18 GiB. The exact non-reparse `target/debug` had zero executing candidate processes; `cargo clean --profile dev` removed 27,841 files/34.6 GiB. `target/debug` is absent afterward, no `target/release` existed, the repository is 23.55 GiB, and the drive has 166.13 GiB free.

Include macOS in the final matched candidate gate. Organization packages remain data only; separately reviewed topology execution remains the sole execution boundary.

- Context: The exact-v27 real updater matrix denied writes only to the installed payload after a valid signed download and verified backup. The baseline desktop/service were then manually relaunched successfully.
- Backup fault evidence: The preceding real backup-directory denial produced the expected visible pre-update backup failure without entering shutdown/install. Deny/restore evidence hashes are `B6E76FDF2FB151B4F31BE17D5EBA0D5A9EA6D5792B881F9FE7E78D574ECBA174` / `F0F0F7CC215285AF6AE0747C96B105228FA6BF699A0D42FD1ED67BA3C3FC92CC`; post-lane snapshot SHA-256 is `411FD1126A9D92F17B5709D21597481BA47113AFB16F5686CC0E6E82AAF5C1D` and retains the baseline. UAT also showed that the simple UI rendered the raw backend SQLite path, motivating the response-body privacy fix below.
- Root cause: `tauri-plugin-updater 2.10.1` calls `ShellExecuteW` and then unconditional `std::process::exit(0)` on Windows. Therefore the existing Rust branch after `update.install(bytes)` can never observe a real NSIS exit or run failure recovery. The real backup-failure UI also exposed a raw SQLite path from the daemon response body.
- Decision/files: Added packaged `mcg-updater` supervisor (`apps/mcg-updater`) and wired it into all sidecar builders, Tauri packaging, and platform package contracts. The desktop now stages only already signature-verified raw NSIS bytes under the private data directory with a canonical request ID/name/SHA-256 and launches only adjacent `mcg-updater.exe`. The supervisor revalidates directory/file/PID/digest identity, waits for desktop exit, runs fixed passive update arguments, waits for the real installer status, removes normal staging residue, and relaunches the prior desktop with a bounded recovery notice on failure. Supervisor-level validation/spawn errors also attempt the fixed adjacent desktop recovery path. Desktop daemon errors now discard response messages and expose only a bounded safe error code, preventing local paths/secrets from reaching simple UI.
- Commands/tests: New supervisor unit tests pass 3/3; a real Windows integration test copies protected system executables into an isolated layout and proves parent wait, real nonzero installer status, bounded outcome, previous-desktop relaunch path, and zero request/installer residue. Desktop Rust tests pass 12/12, desktop Vitest passes 37/37, targeted strict all-target/all-feature Clippy passes, and typecheck/packaging/docs/format/diff checks pass. No GitHub compute ran.
- Follow-up: Build a new signed Windows candidate and repeat the real installer-failure lane to prove packaged recovery and path-free UI. The exact-v27 lane remains valid defect evidence but cannot close production acceptance.

### 2026-08-19 - Standard Git updater archives accepted without weakening type safety

- Context: Independent local updater-contract qualification used a normal `git archive --format=tar.gz` input while the Windows native package build ran unattended.

### 2026-08-18 - Automatic project-memory freshness consolidation is locally complete

- Context: The PRD requires MCG to decide when stale memory should decay or be removed; the existing lifecycle only changed when a user manually pressed a control.
- Decision: Keep automatic policy deterministic and evidence-based. Only an explicit freshness deadline triggers decay; it may decay a previously durable claim because durability records prior usefulness, not perpetual truth. The scheduler examines at most 64 eligible heads per tick, retains a decaying successor for seven days, then records a terminal retracted successor. Concurrent owner edits win. Values, provenance, usefulness/correction counts, predecessor links, and ordinary state-change events remain immutable.
- Files: `crates/mcg-projects/src/memory.rs`, `crates/mcg-storage/src/lib.rs`, `crates/mcg-runtime/src/lib.rs`, `crates/mcg-api/src/lib.rs`, `apps/mcg-cli/src/main.rs`, `apps/desktop/src/{App.tsx,App.test.tsx}`, `tests/e2e/desktop-smoke.spec.ts`, `docs/architecture/project-intelligence-memory.md`, `docs/testing/strategy.md`, `docs/roadmap/production-readiness.md`, and `README.md`.
- Commands/tests: All 82 API, 60 storage, 47 runtime (45 pass plus two environment-only Windows Sandbox ignores), 19 CLI plus two consumer integrations, and eight project tests pass. Strict all-target/all-feature Clippy passes for projects/storage/runtime/API/CLI/Tauri. TypeScript, 33 UI interactions, production frontend build, 6/6 Playwright, docs, formatting, and diff hygiene pass.
- Result: Advanced desktop and CLI accept explicit freshness deadlines; malformed CLI RFC 3339 input fails before daemon contact. Runtime tests prove durable-to-decaying behavior, bounded processing, daemon reopen, seven-day retraction, immutable four-version history, and repeat-run idempotency. API evidence proves automatic consolidation runs even when no user schedule exists. Managed Markdown and fresh context omit expired/decaying facts.
- Follow-up: Exact-qualify this post-v10 slice on Windows/Linux packages and exercise it in real-project UAT. Deeper world-model extraction remains M10 work. Repository size is 46.64 GiB with 24.77 GiB under `target/debug`, below the 50 GB cleanup threshold; no `target/release` exists.

### 2026-08-17 - Definite provider failures settle without false recovery warnings

- Context: Migration 29 safely left every provider error in `started`, but known authentication, rate-limit, HTTP, configuration, and invalid-response failures then appeared permanently active/indeterminate even though retry safety was no longer uncertain.
- Decision: Storage migration 30 adds an immutable terminal `failed` dispatch state with a content-free typed failure class. Confirmed failures settle monotonically before returning; only ambiguous request-transport loss, timeouts, or cancellation retain `started` and the no-resend warning. Diagnostics separately expose started, completed, and failed counts and warn only for started records. No prompt/output, credential, provider/model identity, response body, or private request ID enters the new evidence.

All 38 provider tests, all 56 storage tests, 74 non-browser API tests, and all 30 desktop interactions pass. Strict all-target/all-feature Clippy for providers/agents/storage/runtime/API, TypeScript, formatting, documentation, and diff hygiene pass. The four remaining broad API failures are unchanged restricted-host Chromium Crashpad/Mojo OS-error-5 launch failures.
- Result: A known provider rejection no longer leaves a permanent recovery warning, while a possibly accepted request still cannot be automatically resent. Exact source archive `target/mcg-current-linux-qualification-20260817-v5.tar.gz` contains 249 expected entries, 1,155,645 bytes, SHA-256 `7067DC5BDBC016C9B6610B9E340D457F5AD86ED0AD9C21C2050F1CB337A34BF4`, with no `MEMORY.md`, `.git`, `target`, `tmp`, or `node_modules`. ### 2026-08-17 - Durable physical provider dispatch refuses indeterminate replay

- Context: Stable client request IDs made lost upstream responses traceable but did not prevent a daemon restart from automatically repeating a physical call whose response had not reached its managed checkpoint or terminal ledger.
- Decision: Storage migration 29 adds a content-free append-only physical-dispatch journal keyed by stable client request ID and unique logical call/purpose/ordinal. Every primary/fallback, managed continuation, ensemble member/synthesis, shadow, and streaming request durably enters `started` immediately before network I/O and advances once to `completed` after a validated response. Existing evidence always refuses resend; managed/ordinary calls return HTTP 409, ensemble members become typed `Indeterminate` failures, and shadow records a content-free indeterminate failure. This is at-most-once local dispatch protection, not upstream deduplication or response replay.
- Files: `crates/mcg-providers/src/contracts.rs`, `crates/mcg-agents/src/orchestration.rs`, `crates/mcg-storage/src/{lib,providers}.rs`, `crates/mcg-runtime/src/lib.rs`, `crates/mcg-api/src/{provider_proxy,lib}.rs`, `docs/architecture/provider-proxy.md`, and `docs/testing/strategy.md`.
- Commands/tests: All 37 provider and 54 storage tests pass; focused managed, managed-shadow, direct-shadow, and ensemble restart/adversarial API suites pass; a new close/reopen API fixture seeds an uncheckpointed dispatch, receives HTTP 409 on retry, and proves zero provider requests. Strict all-target/all-feature Clippy passes for providers/agents/storage/runtime/API; formatting, docs, and diff hygiene pass. Full API is 73/77: only the established four restricted-host real-Chromium Crashpad/Mojo OS-error-5 tests fail; all 73 non-browser tests and all changed provider paths pass.
- Result: The response-before-checkpoint crash window can no longer cause an automatic duplicate provider charge or effect for a stable logical call. SQLite triggers prevent deletion, identity mutation, state regression, and repeated completion. Path-validated cleanup removed 11,359 isolated build files and 9.3 GiB (10,038,061,619 measured bytes); repository `target/debug` is absent and `target/release` remains intact. No GitHub-hosted compute ran.
- Follow-up: The v3 archive is superseded by the diagnostic-complete v4 archive recorded above. Run the complete changed-source Ubuntu and matching unrestricted Win11-VM lanes from v4. Keep `mcg:ensemble` undiscoverable until both pass.

### 2026-08-17 - Stable OpenAI client request tracing is distinct from idempotency

- Context: Managed and ensemble checkpoints prevent replay only after a provider response is durably observed. The remaining response-before-checkpoint crash window lacked a stable upstream correlation identity.
- Decision: Give every physical proxy request a deterministic, content-free identity derived from logical call UUID, physical purpose, and ordinal. Send it as bounded `X-Client-Request-Id` only for first-party OpenAI API transport; leave OpenAI subscription OAuth, OpenRouter, local, and arbitrary compatible transports unchanged. Official OpenAI documentation describes this header as request-debugging correlation, not deduplication, so do not call it an idempotency key or treat the crash window as closed.
- Files: `crates/mcg-providers/src/{contracts,compatible}.rs`, `crates/mcg-api/src/provider_proxy.rs`, and `docs/architecture/provider-proxy.md`.
- Commands/tests: All 37 provider library tests; deterministic/distinct/content-free API identity test; managed restart, managed-shadow restart, and ensemble restart integrations; API test compilation; strict all-target/all-feature Clippy for providers/agents/storage/API; formatting; documentation; and diff hygiene pass. The transport fixture proves OpenAI receives the header, the identical arbitrary compatible request does not, and non-ASCII IDs fail before network access. Official source: `https://platform.openai.com/docs/api-reference/debugging-requests`.
- Result: Lost-response support/reconciliation can now use stable physical call identities without exposing prompt/output content or changing non-OpenAI-compatible wire behavior. This is not upstream deduplication; durable pre-dispatch indeterminate-state handling remains the next recovery slice. Exact isolated cleanup removed 9,672 files and 6.7 GiB; repository `target/debug` is absent and `target/release` remains intact. No GitHub-hosted compute ran.
- Follow-up: Durable pre-dispatch refusal is now complete. The v2 and earlier unsuffixed Linux archives are superseded; use only `target/mcg-current-linux-qualification-20260817-v3.tar.gz`, 249 entries, 1,151,220 bytes, SHA-256 `F8000F8343B7F8890628A6E4C07948197A9AEEF77B6062BFFF107CBB0C5966D7`. `MEMORY.md`, `.git`, `target`, `node_modules`, and `tmp` are excluded. Chrome Remote Desktop last showed no Ubuntu host; native SSH still requires an owner-authenticated transfer route.

### 2026-08-16 - Explicit encrypted credential backup and atomic restore implemented

- Context: The PRD requires secrets to remain excluded by default but be exportable through a separately authenticated encrypted backup. The existing schema-1 `.mcgb` path was intentionally secret-free and had no credential import path.

### 2026-08-16 - Baseline-bound automatic shadow research verification

- Context: Automatic shadow assessment supported private-file and isolated command conditions, but `ResearchSources` remained inconclusive even though normal evaluation already retained bounded, content-addressed research evidence. A direct candidate-controlled fetch design was rejected during implementation because it could expand automatic network authority to URLs invented by the candidate.
- Decision: Research shadow assessment is supported only when the exact source evaluation contains a passing matching condition and exactly one re-hashed `governed_research_sources` artifact with the bound scope. The candidate's complete URL set must exactly equal that immutable baseline set. Missing/ambiguous baseline authority remains inconclusive and performs no fetch; URL substitution is a supported failure and performs no fetch. Only then does the credential-rejecting typed verifier refetch the already-authorized URLs and independently check schema, scope, minimum sources, freshness, conflicts, and quote grounding. Research failure summaries omit URL query values.
- Files: `crates/mcg-runtime/src/lib.rs`, `crates/mcg-tools/src/research.rs`, `docs/architecture/experiments.md`, `docs/testing/strategy.md`.
- Commands/tests: Windows passes formatting, all 55 tool tests with one owner-visible handoff test intentionally ignored, all 33 runtime tests with two already-qualified live Windows Sandbox tests intentionally ignored, strict affected all-target/all-feature Clippy, documentation, and diff hygiene. Exact-source Ubuntu hashes match Windows and pass formatting, all 54 tool tests with one visible-handoff test ignored, all 31 runtime tests including live Bubblewrap, strict affected Clippy, and documentation. The full automatic promotion/restart scenario now retains and reuses a real baseline research artifact across reopen; focused authorization tests prove one valid fetch and zero additional requests for scope tampering, private-URL substitution, malformed JSON, or missing baseline binding.
- Result: Automatic candidates can now pass or fail research objectives from independently refetched, immutable, baseline-authorized evidence without granting the candidate agent credentials or new network origins. Source/documentation commit `dfb1551` is on private `main`. Exact Ubuntu qualification staging and the 4.8 MiB local transfer directory were removed. After push, the validated 5.90 GiB `target/debug` cache had zero executing processes; Cargo removed 8,096 files (5.9 GiB), `target/debug` is absent, `target/release` remains intact, and the repository measures 7.10 GiB. No GitHub-hosted compute ran.
- Follow-up: Design the isolated web/service verifier path for the remaining HTTP/browser objective kinds and continue owner-present Ubuntu/macOS OAuth parity.

### 2026-08-16 - Full Win11 Windows Sandbox qualification corrected and passed

- Context: The first negative-gate run exposed concurrent ignored tests racing the Store package's single-instance application contract. After serializing live fixtures, raw `wsb exec --raw` evidence showed the host CLI exited zero while its JSON `ExitCode` was `-1073741515` (`0xC0000135`): the dynamically linked Rust runner could not start because pristine Windows Sandbox lacks `VCRUNTIME140.dll`. This also proves the earlier positive gate at `52d541e` was a false positive for nested execution; it qualified lifecycle/host mapping but incorrectly trusted wrapper status.
- Decision: Serialize all live Sandbox fixtures. Treat the host CLI process status only as transport status and require a bounded JSON `ExitCode` envelope for every runner exec. Build the Windows runner separately with `-C target-feature=+crt-static`, reject `VCRUNTIME`/`MSVCP` DLL imports during sidecar preparation, and preserve unconditional session teardown even when postflight fails.
- Files: `crates/mcg-runtime/src/shadow_verification.rs`, `scripts/prepare-sidecars.ps1`, `scripts/check-packaging.mjs`, `docs/architecture/experiments.md`, `docs/architecture/packaging.md`, `docs/testing/strategy.md`.
- Commands/tests: Final Windows source passes formatting, 30 runtime tests with two live tests ignored by default, three runner tests, strict affected Clippy, documentation, packaging, and diff hygiene. Full debug sidecar preparation succeeds and emits the same static runner. Exact-source Ubuntu passes formatting, 30 runtime tests including live Bubblewrap, three runner tests, strict affected Clippy, documentation, and packaging. The exact final 46,680,576-byte runtime test executable has SHA-256 `95617B34506C6D7A843A4C15FB7CC0E3D42155C8E6E3A907BB6870D83453E59D`; the exact 714,240-byte self-contained runner has SHA-256 `651CD3C1A78EC10004168CE4E2D48B107A35BC9AA41ECA2EC8CC2B56E83692AC` and imports only `api-ms-win-core-synch-l1-2-0.dll`, `KERNEL32.dll`, and `ntdll.dll`.
- Result: The exact final-source hash-gated Win11-VM lane passed both live tests in 48.37 seconds. It proves real nested runner execution, an ephemeral private workspace, no host persistence through read-only mappings, no default IPv4 route or non-loopback IPv4 address, a two-second host timeout followed by verified session teardown, deliberate schema-invalid setup rejection, and an empty Sandbox session list after every case. The exploratory/final scoped cleanups removed 18 then four transferred artifacts, each left zero matching artifacts and independently reported `WindowsSandboxEnvironments: []`; both helpers self-deleted. The exact Ubuntu qualification tree and 274.4 MiB local transfer staging were removed. No host fallback or GitHub-hosted compute ran.
- Follow-up: Completed in commit `87c59b5` and the post-push cleanup entry above; resume the remaining M9 verifier/package gates and Ubuntu/macOS normal browser OAuth parity.

### 2026-08-16 - Live Win11 Windows Sandbox lifecycle and host-isolation gate passed

- Context: Win11-VM was upgraded to the current Microsoft Store-delivered Windows Sandbox CLI (`MicrosoftWindows.WindowsSandbox` 0.8.107.0). The existing detector assumed `System32\wsb.exe`; the live gate then exposed Windows canonical `\\?\` path spelling, a Store CLI contract that rejects a POSIX-style `--` argument tail, and a test assumption that read-only mapped-folder writes must fail rather than remain disposable.
- Decision: Prefer a fixed trusted System32 CLI when present; otherwise discover exactly one official Store package through fixed System32 Windows PowerShell/Appx under a minimal environment. Accept only its publisher-qualified direct child of `Program Files\WindowsApps` and non-reparse/nonempty real CLI. Normalize only the Windows verbatim prefix, separators, and ASCII case when comparing a canonical fixed path. Give `wsb exec` one of two compile-time runner command strings (probe or request); keep every untrusted objective executable/argument in the bounded JSON request. Measure read-only mapping security by host non-persistence because Windows Sandbox may satisfy a write through its disposable view.
- Files: `crates/mcg-runtime/src/shadow_verification.rs`, `docs/architecture/experiments.md`, `docs/testing/strategy.md`, `packaging/windows/portable/README.txt`.
- Commands/tests: Windows passed 29 runtime unit tests with the live gate intentionally ignored by default, three runner tests, strict all-target/all-feature runtime/runner Clippy, formatting, documentation and packaging validation, and diff hygiene. The exact 46,638,080-byte live test executable had SHA-256 `6C1E189FC54ACBA21AC6BBC80C37A870EC68A9CC3CB9524356F67323476E3B65`; the exact 614,400-byte runner had SHA-256 `D00D07F09F9AC5E9B7019885142DDD14B1D72433A8040E8A1FE347234F402023`. Their hash-gated Win11 launcher ran `installed_windows_sandbox_executes_ephemerally_and_keeps_host_mappings_read_only` successfully in 10.74 seconds (1 passed, exit success). Scoped cleanup then reported 19 transferred artifacts removed, zero remaining artifacts, zero matching temporary firewall rules, and an empty `WindowsSandboxEnvironments` list; the helper/result self-deleted after inspection. About 268 MiB of matching local transfer staging was also removed. After pushing exact code/documentation commit `52d541e`, path-validated `cargo clean --target-dir <repository>/target/debug` removed 7,575 regenerated files (4.7 GiB); `target/debug` is absent, `target/release` is preserved, and the repository measures 7.03 GiB.

### 2026-08-16 - Terminal agent-turn accounting is lossless

- Context: Failed provider turns, denied approval-waiting turns, and task cancellation deleted durable turn checkpoints even when those checkpoints contained real paid model calls and completed governed tools. Task cancellation also left the associated owner approval pending.
- Decision: Share one validated checkpoint-accounting path across successful and terminal settlement. Before deleting continuation state, atomically persist immutable provider/tool usage, update run and lease metrics, and retain the assigned route. Never estimate usage absent from provider evidence and never create a successful result for failed, denied, or cancelled work. Task cancellation now cancels every still-pending approval in the same transaction.
- Files: `crates/mcg-storage/src/agents.rs`, `crates/mcg-runtime/src/lib.rs`, `crates/mcg-api/src/lib.rs`, `docs/architecture/agent-organization.md`, `docs/architecture/runtime-spine.md`, `docs/testing/strategy.md`.
- Commands/tests: Windows passed 44 storage, 28 runtime, and 56 API tests. Exact commit `6d4353bf6385d23624b98e764fa03fdb675d69e6` then passed 44 storage, 29 runtime, and 56 API tests on Ubuntu, including live Bubblewrap, the AppArmor-confined packaged Chromium, and the official TypeScript MCP SDK; strict all-target/all-feature Clippy for all three crates, formatting, documentation validation, diff hygiene, lockfile-exact `npm ci`, and a zero-vulnerability npm audit pass. The exact 1,128,525-byte source archive matched SHA-256 `c293d6c346855810a293ed1eae326a9231308c49c99b246b98f60c3cf60f6647` across hosts. The provider-failure regression retains one 60-token call plus one completed tool while producing no result. The public guided-consumer scenario proves denial retains one 48-token call, retry cancellation retains a second exactly once, both checkpoints disappear, the active lease terminates, and the pending approval becomes cancelled. Initial Ubuntu attempts that omitted the user-local Node path/`node_modules` or selected unconfined Playwright Chromium are invalid environment evidence; the corrected full lane is green.
- Result: Real model/tool spend can no longer disappear from run evidence when a turn terminates unsuccessfully, and cancelled tasks leave no resumable checkpoint or stale pending approval. No GitHub-hosted compute ran.
- Follow-up: Resume normal browser OAuth/provider lifecycle parity on Ubuntu and macOS plus the remaining final release gates.

### 2026-08-20 - Typed TCP listener objective and isolated benchmark verifier

- Context: The PRD's system-administration success examples require proving that a port is listening, but the objective catalog previously required an HTTP endpoint and could not honestly verify non-HTTP services.
- Decision: Add `PortListening { endpoint }` with exact consumer syntax `PORT LISTENING tcp://host:port`, backed by a server-owned `tcp.connect` tool request. Endpoints require a credential-free TCP scheme, explicit nonzero port, and no path/query/fragment. The probe performs only a timeout-bounded handshake, sends no application bytes, captures no artifact, and uses the normal exact network capability, lease, approval, audit, replay, and evidence path.
- Isolation: Candidate benchmarks accept only exact loopback TCP endpoints. Linux performs the connect-only probe beside the candidate service in the same Bubblewrap network namespace; Windows runner schema 3 performs it inside the disposable Windows Sandbox. Both retain the existing zero host-network/process fallback and fail-closed evidence rules. The ignored live Linux/Win11 qualification fixtures now include the TCP lane.
- Files: `crates/mcg-{agents,tools,runtime,api}/`, `apps/mcg-shadow-runner/`, `README.md`, and objective/experiment/testing/roadmap documentation.
- Commands/tests: Full affected suites pass: 22 agent, 60 storage, 56 active tool (one ignored), 48 active runtime (two ignored live sandbox tests), 83 API, and six serial shadow-runner tests. Strict affected all-target/all-feature Clippy with warnings denied, formatting, documentation validation, and diff hygiene pass from the external target. The consumer integration proves typed parsing, governed completion, artifact-free evidence, and no-effect terminal replay; tool/broker tests prove exact capability confinement, malformed endpoint rejection, successful handshake, and refusal failure.
- Cleanup: With zero Cargo/rustc processes and both exact paths validated, `cargo clean` removed 17,866 files / 18.6 GiB (`19,946,623,758` measured bytes) from the external target; profile-scoped Cargo cleanup removed 359 files / 90.5 MiB (`94,922,018` measured bytes) from repository `target/debug`. Both are absent/empty and `target/release` remains absent.
- Follow-up: Source and evidence markers are pushed through `8608cd9`; execute the regenerated exact-current Win11 isolation bundle and retain its terminal evidence.

## Current State

- MCG now has a compilable implementation foundation alongside its product specifications.
- The complete vision remains in scope and will be delivered through integrated, end-to-end milestones rather than a throwaway narrow prototype.
- Canonical requirements are indexed by `MCG_PRD.md` and split across `docs/spec/`; the encoding-damaged source is retained at `docs/archive/MCG_PRD.original.md`.
- The Git repository uses `main`, with `origin` at `https://github.com/fourtytwo42/Metacognitive-Governor.git`; the GitHub repository is private (last verified 2026-08-13).
- The workspace contains shared Rust crates, runnable `mcg-daemon` and `mcg` CLI applications, and a Tauri 2/React desktop shell with simple/advanced modes.
- The consumer CLI supports interactive no-argument use, bare quoted goals, project selection with `-C`, bounded piped standard-input context, one-document JSON results, and task-filtered NDJSON event streaming. It reuses the durable project identity for a canonical path, submits through the same durable consumer launch contract as the desktop, reconciles fast streams from authoritative history, and returns nonzero for blocked, failed, or approval-waiting outcomes.
- Native production packaging now has a release-only Tauri overlay so clean development/test builds do not depend on generated binaries. Local Windows/Linux/macOS builders refuse dirty trees, build target-matched CLI/service/sandbox sidecars, include third-party notices plus the full Apache-2.0 and Wasmtime Apache-2.0-with-LLVM-exceptions texts, explicitly declare square cross-platform icons, create NSIS, `.deb`/AppImage, or native app/DMG packages, and emit a commit-bound SHA-256 manifest. The reviewed Wasmtime 45.0.3 license payload is digest-pinned by the package contract. A separate signing-only overlay creates Tauri updater artifacts only with a complete external endpoint/public-key/private-key environment; unsigned builds fail closed. The updater rechecks versions, verifies signed packages, verifies a pre-update backup, stops the daemon, installs, and restarts; shutdown and installer failures attempt and visibly report service recovery. The desktop authenticates an exact-version loopback service, launches only adjacent `mcg-service`, pins child bind/data environment, reaps every launched child, and shows actionable startup failure. Windows uses current-user installation; repair/uninstall preserve external app data. Owner-built installed-package repair/restart/CLI/doctor/backup/MCP/GUI/screenshot/uninstall/data-retention/reinstall UAT is qualified on Windows, Linux, and macOS; exact v27 additionally passes real Linux signed-AppImage shutdown-timeout and ENOSPC rollback. Trusted production signing/notarization, updater publication/full cross-platform fault UAT, and one final signed matched-candidate rerun remain release gates.
- `scripts/test-release-soak.mjs` is a cross-platform optimized-daemon performance/soak/forced-crash gate. It admits bounded concurrent authenticated tasks, enforces startup/p95/throughput/RSS limits, continuously probes health, force-terminates the daemon, reopens the same SQLite store, verifies every task plus sampled exact event history, and requires matching diagnostic counts with passing database/schema checks. Fast, one-hour, and matched exact-v27 two-hour/10,000-task lanes are qualified on Windows and Ubuntu with atomic hash-bound terminal evidence and zero residual candidate processes.
- `mcg-plugins` now defines the PRD's two plugin trust classes. Trusted-native plugins are statically linked, manifest-validated, duplicate-safe, and exactly routed; dynamic-library loading remains excluded under the workspace's unsafe-code ban. Sandboxed plugins use pinned Wasmtime/WASIp1 45.0.3 with bounded JSON streams, module/input/output/memory/table/instance/fuel/wall limits, async cancellation, no inherited environment/filesystem/network/terminal authority, and unknown-import denial. The lightweight `mcg-plugin-protocol` keeps Wasmtime out of the daemon-side dependency shape. `mcg-plugin-runner` adds a one-request OS process and bounded package-local parent client. Migration 26 durably stores immutable module revisions, optimistic enabled heads, scoped grants, hash-only invocation history, and non-replayable restart uncertainty. Content-addressed runtime installation/replay and canonical Ed25519 registry trust are complete. Strict owner grants plus an eight-request host/guest protocol route task-project filesystem, exact-origin HTTP, exact executable process, and exact provider/key references through normal policy, approvals, leases, redaction, artifacts, and immutable audit; declared capabilities never create ambient WASI authority. Authenticated REST is canonical; CLI, Tauri, simple/advanced desktop, and bounded MCP management/invocation adapters share the contract. Portable backup/restore verifies and retains plugin catalog/module/result/history evidence and disables restored plugins for explicit reauthorization. License/package qualification, cross-platform adversarial execution, clean Win11-VM installed-package UAT, and Ubuntu `.deb`/AppImage UAT pass.
- Schema 34 implements opt-in signed organization sharing as a separate data-only trust boundary. Exact canonical HTTPS registry scope, immutable Ed25519 publisher keys, unique keys, terminal revocation, signature/domain separation, immutable review evidence, and distinct reasoned import/reject mutations are enforced durably. REST, four MCP tools, CLI, seven Tauri commands, and simple/advanced desktop UI share the contract. Exact `26353f2` sharing/restart UAT and corrective exact `8f50b99` source/package/installed qualification pass on Win11 and Ubuntu; focused native Win11 UAT also proves the empty-origin invariant plus visible-browser navigate/handoff/privacy-blanked reclaim/history/close. Final matched macOS inclusion remains.
- Diagnostic report schema 1 is authenticated, explicit, read-only, and content-free. It combines SQLite quick-check/schema/count evidence, regular auth-token file validation, full portable-backup verification, provider/model and optional MCP/platform inventories, durable task/schedule/approval counts, and separate started/completed/failed physical-dispatch counts. Only started dispatches warn as active/indeterminate. REST, `mcg doctor`, Tauri, and desktop Settings share stable check codes/severities; the CLI strict mode fails on warnings, and diagnostics never probe a provider or expose paths, connection IDs, credentials, prompts, outputs, task goals, event payloads, or artifact bytes.
- Portable backup schema 2 retains schema-1 reading and keeps routine creation secret-free, bounded, deterministic, atomic/no-clobber, and content-addressed. An explicit Advanced/CLI/API mode encrypts the exact provider/MCP credential catalog with bounded Argon2id plus authenticated XChaCha20-Poly1305; the installation token is always excluded. Verification rejects traversal, links, duplicates, malformed digests, truncation, trailing data, changed sources, excessive sizes, inconsistent secret claims, crypto-parameter/tamper/binding failures, plugin catalog/module mismatches, missing successful invocation results, malformed projections, and inconsistent invocation history. Offline restore refuses future schemas and WAL/SHM ambiguity, preserves the destination auth token, merges only hash-matching artifacts, disables restored plugin heads, and retains `backups/pre-restore-*.sqlite3`. Explicit `--restore-secrets` authenticates the passphrase, requires exact connection/auth-mode binding, preserves unrelated credentials, and rolls protected slots back on any installation failure. Authenticated REST, `mcg backup`, Tauri, and desktop Settings share creation/list/verification; restore remains offline. Fresh exact-source installed-package encrypted create/verify/wrong-passphrase/correct-restore/restart UAT passes on both Windows and Linux.
- Linux daemon shutdown listens for both SIGINT and SIGTERM so a normal service stop reaches Axum graceful shutdown, drops the runtime, and removes SQLite WAL/SHM sidecars before offline maintenance. Corrected `.deb`/AppImage UAT requires this invariant before backup restore.
- Migration 25 persists owner settings independently from startup-only trust roots. Simple/advanced mode, Guided/Balanced/Autonomous policy, and shadow-experiment opt-in update live with optimistic revisions, immutable reasons/timestamps, and restart durability. REST, `mcg settings`, Tauri, and the desktop Settings workspace share the contract; each in-flight authority decision uses one settings snapshot.
- A Windows portable qualification layout keeps `mcg-desktop.exe`, `mcg-daemon.exe`, and `mcg.exe` distinct and launches the daemon plus GUI without requiring a developer toolchain. Production installer/signing/upgrade/uninstall work remains a later release gate.
- A matching Linux portable qualification layout keeps `mcg-desktop`, `mcg-daemon`, and `mcg` distinct. Exact Ubuntu qualification archive `MCG-0.1.0-linux-x64-portable.tar.gz` has SHA-256 `29B95A3E19C73F803BBAFF4ACC168D3EBCF8231DF8264D52D3FB716993F7D61F`; production package/signing/desktop-integration/service/upgrade/uninstall gates remain open.
- The desktop task view loads authoritative event history and immutable governed-tool audit together. Simple mode summarizes outcomes, checks, tool runs, artifacts, and condition results; advanced mode adds ordered events, resources, verification records, and artifact hashes. Any retained run can be deterministically replay-verified from Evidence, displaying invariant count, no-side-effect status, and the exact SHA-256 report digest.
- The desktop provider workspace supports OpenAI subscription OAuth, OpenAI API keys, OpenRouter, and arbitrary OpenAI-compatible/local endpoints. It discovers before saving, stores credentials through the existing OS credential service, labels billing explicitly, and displays known price metadata or `Cost unknown`. Anonymous provider disconnect bypasses the OS credential service entirely; authenticated/OAuth disconnect retains protected credential deletion and rollback.
- The OpenAI-compatible proxy uses migration 24 for durable secret-free conversation/call accounting, migration 27/schema-2 private monotonic managed-turn checkpoints, migration 28 private ensemble checkpoints, migration 29 for an append-only content-free physical-dispatch journal, and migration 30 for terminal content-free failure classification. Every physical request is claimed before network I/O; a missing checkpoint after restart is an explicit indeterminate refusal and is never automatically resent. Authentication, rate-limit, HTTP, configuration, and invalid-response failures settle as `failed`; only ambiguous request-transport loss, timeout, or cancellation remains `started`. Reserved metadata UUIDs attach governed project/task context and are stripped before provider transport; project-bound requests inject a bounded versioned assembly with a hash-only receipt. Tool ownership is exact: unqualified/`caller::` is returned untouched, while canonical `host::` and `mcg::` calls require explicit task/conversation/call UUIDs. Managed host calls use the normal project-confined approval/lease/redaction/audit broker; `mcg::task.get`, `mcg::project.context`, and `mcg::memory.search` are bounded read-only and audited. Same-process duplicate identities serialize, restart resumes paid/model/tool evidence, terminal outcomes atomically retain real usage and remove continuation authority, and only final buffered or synthesized Responses/Chat output crosses the API. Direct shadow separately requires both opt-ins, stable IDs, and a distinct secondary; ordinary, caller-tool, and MCG-managed-tool turns discard secondary content/tools and record only content-free evidence, with managed shadow authority pinned monotonically across approval/restart. Terminal replay is refused because prompt/output content is intentionally absent from the ledger. REST, `mcg provider usage/conversations`, Tauri, and desktop expose the same history and known/unknown cost totals.
- Proxy aliases `mcg:fast` and `mcg:best` are implemented without trusting names: fast requires at least three successful local duration samples and ranks p95, while best ranks discovered true capabilities then context. Content-free proxy ledgers retain bounded duration and read older records as duration-unknown. The migration-28 `mcg:ensemble` engine now passes its local execution/adversarial matrix but remains intentionally absent from `/v1/models` until exact changed-source Windows/Linux qualification passes.
- The desktop Projects panel lists durable discovered workspaces with language/build/test/Git context and exposes paths, branches, and instruction-conflict counts only in advanced mode. Selected projects also expose simple provenance-bearing fact capture; advanced mode adds confidence, correction/reinforcement/decay/retraction, immutable history, the discovered world graph, and managed-memory Markdown through the canonical daemon contract.
- Project discovery builds a bounded stable provenance topology from package/Cargo/Python/Go, Docker Compose, OpenAPI/Swagger, and common deployment manifests. Named services, databases, dependencies, runtimes, API origins, deployments, commands, and declared edges retain manifest sources; credential-bearing URLs and oversized/malformed manifests fail closed. Advanced desktop resolves internal IDs to readable node labels. The same readable topology enters REST/CLI/MCP/provider-proxy and worker/evaluator context under caller-selected bounds; dependency/database edges outrank isolated inventory during compression.
- Project-memory freshness is deterministic: optional deadlines are owner-visible through advanced desktop and CLI, each scheduler tick processes at most 64 eligible heads, expired active claims become decaying successors, and decaying claims become terminal retracted successors after seven days. The version chain and state events survive reopen; concurrent owner changes are never overwritten.
- The desktop Improvement workspace shows immutable supervision signals, candidate experiment state, evidence counts, protected baseline language, and promotion/reversion outcomes in simple mode. Advanced mode exposes complete versioned contracts and legal state advances; REST, `mcg experiment`, MCP, and Tauri use the same evidence gates and caller identities.
- M9 automatic supervision analyzes only durable run/evaluation evidence. Every daemon scheduler tick drains up to 32 unsupervised evaluations; migration 21 records an immutable exactly-once marker even when no signal is produced, and processed evaluations replay their stored result rather than recomputing against later mutable run projections.
- M9 candidate orchestration is enabled by default in development/test builds and opt-in in releases through `MCG_SHADOW_EXPERIMENTS`. The effective value is authenticated status/CLI-visible. When enabled, scheduler ticks create at most one active automatic candidate experiment per organization; regression candidates require independent evaluation, raise the confidence floor, and permit bounded Coder/Evaluator model escalation. Candidate/draft partial commits resume safely and the baseline stays active.
- Inactive organization revisions are an enforced evaluation-only authority class. Automatic candidates may receive only role-appropriate filesystem primitives rooted at a durable private copy; MCP, network, browser, system, process, credentials, the source project, and real task settlement remain unavailable. Public/non-shadow dispatch of an inactive revision fails closed, and storage accepts candidate tool outcomes only with a matching running experiment and ready workspace.
- Automatic ready/running experiments without a receipt map to one digest-derived candidate run. Planning reuses copied task goals and conditions but resolves roles against the candidate and requires an independent Evaluator; planned/recovering runs advance to running and resume after reopen without creating a second paid-work identity.
- Automatic shadow dispatch is sequential and isolated: migration 23 claims `shadow-workspaces/{experiment_id}` before copying, applies file/depth/size bounds, rejects symlinks/non-regular entries, skips `.git` and nested app data, hashes source and copied content, marks failed/crash-interrupted preparation `uncertain`, and atomically moves a receipted workspace from `ready` to non-runnable `retained`. Missing routes are a no-spend no-op, and a work item with any non-completed prior lease is never retried automatically. Complete result JSON is retained in the local content-addressed artifact store before the evaluation-only receipt is recorded.
- Automatic assessment maps the source observation back to its immutable evaluation, requires a completed candidate Evaluator, records stable candidate evaluation/benchmark/decision identities, and content-addresses both evaluation arms. Provider/filesystem-loop evidence is real, but any condition lacking an objective-specific isolated verifier remains explicitly inconclusive and rejected; restart after benchmark commit resumes the decision and preserves the baseline head.
- `FileExists` is the first condition-specific automatic-shadow verifier. It reopens only the retained private root, rejects unsafe relative paths and symlink escapes, captures a bounded regular file as immutable evidence, and checks minimum size plus optional SHA-256. All-supported passes can now promote from real evidence; a supported failure is `failed`, while any unsupported condition keeps the evaluation `inconclusive`.
- `ResearchSources` automatic assessment requires a passing source condition plus exactly one verified baseline report with the bound scope. Candidate source URLs must equal that immutable baseline set exactly; missing authority stays inconclusive, changed authority fails without a request, and the credential-rejecting verifier then independently refetches and grounds only the previously authorized sources. Query values never enter failure details.
- Build/test/static-analysis and exact custom-command shadow objectives cross a dedicated `ShadowCommandVerifier` boundary. Unsupported Windows/Linux hosts remain unavailable and execute nothing. Linux selects only root-owned, non-setuid/non-setgid, non-writable bubblewrap 0.11.2+ with `prlimit`; it copies a bounded read-only retained source into an ephemeral size-limited tmpfs under explicitly unshared network/user/PID/IPC/UTS/cgroup namespaces, clears environment, drops capabilities, disables nested user namespaces, and applies CPU/address-space/file/process/FD/wall/output bounds. Windows prefers a fixed trusted System32 Windows Sandbox CLI or resolves exactly the official Store package beneath protected `WindowsApps`; it never trusts PATH or the app alias. It starts one serialized UUID session with networking/devices/redirections disabled and Protected Client enabled, maps retained source and harness read-only, copies through bounded link/reparse-safe code into an unmapped disposable workspace, gives the CLI only compile-time probe/request runner strings, and lets the statically linked runner execute the untrusted direct argument vector as System with a cleared environment. It treats host CLI status only as transport and requires the bounded raw nested `ExitCode`; setup/command/timeout states cross pre/post probes and every path stops the VM best-effort. Neither backend has a host fallback. Trusted runtime reopens/re-hashes the exact-condition transcript and derives pass/fail itself. Exact Win11 positive and blocked-network/timeout/setup-failure qualification passes with an empty session list after every case.
- Automatic `HttpResponds`, `ServiceHealthy`, `PortListening`, `PageRenders`, `ElementExists`, `Accessibility`, and `BrowserWorkflow` shadow assessment is bound to the completed source run's retained checkpoint and immutable project snapshot. One exact `start` script is preferred, otherwise one exact `dev`; ambiguity or missing durable authority stays inconclusive. Runner schema 7 starts the service and performs bounded credential-free loopback HTTP/TCP/browser probes in the same Linux Bubblewrap namespace or disposable Windows Sandbox VM. Page, bounded CSS-element, shared versioned accessibility, and digest-bound typed workflow assertions use a bounded read-only Chromium distribution and DevTools client inside that boundary. Exact status/2xx/listener/render/selector/accessibility/workflow evidence passes; mismatch, timeout, changed workflow bytes, unsafe interaction, service/browser exit, invalid input, failed audit, or malformed evidence fails, and trusted runtime revalidates the versioned transcript.
- Automatic promotion refuses benchmark arms unless revision, run, evaluation outcome/confidence, and run metrics match durable evidence. Reversion decisions include explicit trigger evaluation IDs; storage proves each is a post-promotion failure/regression on the candidate revision before atomically restoring the baseline. Scheduler scans are idempotent after reversion.
- GitHub Actions is disabled at repository level and its workflow/Dependabot files are removed; GitHub is source hosting only.
- M1 task state is durable in SQLite, rebuilt from its authoritative event log at startup, protected by a persistent local bearer token, and exposed through authenticated REST plus resumable SSE.
- M6 has an authenticated stateless MCP `2026-07-28` Streamable HTTP server with deterministic read/query tools, complete consumer task lifecycle controls, and an idempotent governed single-turn mutation. `mcg_run_task` uses the desktop/CLI organization, decomposition, independent evaluation, objective verification, approval, replay, and terminal-settlement path. Task queries page events by sequence and tool audit by offset; continuation delegates to shared retry/resume; cancellation atomically settles task/run/leases; external evaluation reports are identity/evidence validated and replay-safe. Opt-in request-scoped SSE progress uses caller string/number tokens, publishes only SQLite-backed task event kind/sequence, ends with the normal tool result, and treats response closure as cancellation. Its client supports current/legacy HTTP and stdio configuration/discovery/calls through API, CLI, and desktop. The pinned official TypeScript SDK interoperates against a real loopback server. Server tools are fixed for a daemon build and MCG advertises no prompts/resources, so `subscriptions/listen` is deliberately deferred until a catalog becomes runtime-mutable; native Windows/Linux UAT remains the M6 release gate.
- Consumer Start requires a selected durable project and uses migration 10 to bind each existing task to exactly one running consumer plan. API, CLI, Tauri, and desktop share the launch contract; retries never reissue model work, and model self-report remains insufficient for completion.
- Guided local-tool approvals can be reviewed and resumed from the desktop task card. Resume revalidates durable authority, the exact approval, current provider/tool metadata, and unchanged project context; stable request identity and the provider-turn checkpoint prevent duplicate inference and tool side effects.
- Provider/tool turns checkpoint bounded credential-free private execution state after each provider response and before/after governed tool calls. Waiting local or external MCP authority survives restart only with a matching live checkpoint/approval/lease; resume continues the pinned conversation without replaying paid inference, and terminal authority removes the checkpoint atomically.
- Migrations 13-14 store one-time/interval/UTC-cron/event/file-watch/adaptive-heartbeat definitions, occurrence history, subsystem wakes, and notifications. The daemon atomically admits one active occurrence per schedule, persists cursors/fingerprints, prevents self-trigger loops, immediately reclaims interrupted identity after restart, and dispatches through consumer launch. Preflight budget exhaustion and approval/manual-evaluation waits pause recurring admission; REST, CLI, Tauri, and the desktop dashboard expose creation, history, wake, notification, and state controls without deleting history.
- Schedule creation accepts a caller-owned UUID and stores a server-generated SHA-256 definition hash. Exact retries replay one definition (`201` first REST call, `200` replay); changed input conflicts, duplicate storage insertion is atomic, malformed non-empty hashes fail closed, the desktop preserves identity across lost responses, CLI accepts optional `--schedule-id`, and MCP exposes authenticated create/list/remove tools that retain history.
- M7 integrated exit evidence drives a real guided project-file watch through baseline/change, consumer provider work, exact objective approval, restart recovery, resume, three governed build/test/check executions, terminal occurrence reconciliation/notification, and an additional scheduler tick with one task, two model calls, and no duplicate tool completion.
- M8 uses `PLATFORM_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1` and migration 15 for immutable deduplicated platform-capability snapshots plus durable browser/computer session projections and immutable lifecycle events. Session creation requires a caller UUID and exact SHA-256 definition hash; exact replay is idempotent, conflicting reuse fails, authority custody is explicit, unsafe credential-bearing origins fail closed, expired live reservations close exactly once, and startup converts remaining live authority to `interrupted` with retained history. The persistent browser layer now proves launch before activation, retains one isolated ephemeral Chromium process/page, intercepts traffic under immutable origin and method grants, denies downloads, redacts query/fragment data, supports visible-only local handoff and privacy-blanked reclaim, and coordinates close/expiry cleanup across REST, CLI, MCP, Tauri, and desktop.
- Migration 16 stores non-deletable computer action batches. New actions are claimed as `executing` before host input and settle as `succeeded`, `rejected`, or `uncertain`; exact successful replay is observation-only, conflicting reuse fails, and startup converts crash-interrupted claims to non-replayable `uncertain`. Durable hashes exclude typed text and pixels. Browser reservation hashes remain compatible across the additive computer fields.
- Computer input uses an exact-session capability plus the same durable policy/approval/audit/lease broker as other host mutations. Guided approval is committed before action-ledger claim or host input. Caller-owned session/batch UUIDs are mapped to namespaced stable synthetic authority/request identities, preventing cross-domain collisions while preserving exact approval retry.
- Sites execute bounded same-origin health GETs and structured deployment processes through the shared broker. Publication requires approval even in Autonomous mode before a deployment record or process exists; output is retained as artifacts, exact retries are observation-only, and startup converts claimed work to non-replayable uncertainty. Revision-checked changes retain immutable history and archive is terminal. One durable scheduler-backed interval monitor survives restart, uses occurrence IDs as health-check replay IDs, atomically settles monitor state/transition notifications, coalesces repeated outages, and synchronizes pause/resume/archive with schedule state. REST, CLI, MCP, Tauri, and desktop share the same registry and identities.
- Consumer plans whose conditions are entirely discovered build/test/static-analysis commands now execute those checks through the governed broker after independent Evaluator completion. Validated reports and completed/failed task/run projections commit atomically; terminal Start replay is observation-only.
- Guided command evaluation stores its run/project/condition progress in SQLite and requests one approval for the immutable discovered batch. Denial blocks for bounded retry with a new request identity; approved work resumes across daemon restart without another provider turn or repeated completed check.
- Advanced consumer condition lines accept exact `HTTP STATUS URL` syntax. Runtime grants only the parsed credential-free origin, performs a body-bounded governed GET, captures response bytes as an artifact, and compares observed status exactly; status mismatch, timeout, and connection failure become failed evaluation evidence.
- Advanced consumer condition lines accept exact `SERVICE HEALTHY URL` syntax. Runtime grants only the credential-free endpoint origin and performs the same bounded governed GET; final 2xx passes, reachable 4xx/5xx fails with an immutable response artifact, and connection/timeout failure retains no fabricated response evidence. Terminal replay repeats neither inference nor the network check.
- Advanced consumer condition lines accept exact `PAGE RENDERS URL` syntax. Runtime launches a fresh local Chromium CDP session, permits only exact-origin GET/HEAD plus in-page data/blob resources, checks DOM readiness/layout after JavaScript, and captures rendered HTML plus a PNG. Missing browser runtime blocks as inconclusive; render failure is objective failure.
- Advanced consumer condition lines accept exact `ACCESSIBILITY CHECK URL` syntax. The fresh same-origin Chromium executor audits the hydrated DOM using MCG accessibility baseline v1, artifacts the complete versioned JSON report plus rendered HTML/PNG, and fails exact high-confidence language/title/landmark/name/alt/ID/heading/tab-order violations. This reduces regression work without claiming full WCAG or assistive-technology conformance.
- Advanced consumer conditions accept `ELEMENT EXISTS URL selector` and `BROWSER WORKFLOW project/relative/file.json`. Workflows are immutable, versioned JSON with 1-64 typed assertion/click/type/key steps; arbitrary JavaScript is excluded. The executor revalidates file bytes, confines all page traffic and navigation to same-origin GET/HEAD, rejects downloads/new contexts/cross-origin targets/mutating forms, and retains JSON, final HTML, final PNG, and ordered step evidence.
- Advanced consumer condition lines accept `FILE EXISTS project/relative/path` and exact `COMMAND SUCCEEDS discovered command`. File evidence is regular-file-only, capability-confined, streamed with a 1 GiB ceiling, and records artifact size/SHA-256 without stdout. Custom commands execute only when the complete command still matches safe current project discovery; arbitrary prose remains inconclusive.
- Advanced consumer condition lines accept `SCREENSHOT MATCHES URL project/relative/baseline.png`. Launch hashes the regular baseline before model work; evaluation revalidates that digest, renders under exact-origin browser confinement, decodes bounded PNGs, and applies explicit channel/changed-area tolerances. Missing baselines fail before spend; dimension, visual, or post-binding baseline changes fail the objective.
- Advanced consumer condition lines accept `RESEARCH EVIDENCE project/relative/report.json`. Runtime captures and hashes bounded JSON under governed file authority, binds its scope to the exact task goal, derives and authorizes only declared credential-free source origins, then fetches at most 32 sources under one timeout with 1 MiB per-response limits. It structurally validates claims, grounded short quotes, optional freshness, and conflict references; the independent Evaluator retains semantic responsibility. JSON and fetched bodies are immutable artifacts, and terminal replay performs no model or network work.

## User Preferences

- Keep `MEMORY.md` updated consistently with durable context that should survive chat compression.
- Agents should read `AGENTS.md` at startup and check `MEMORY.md` whenever context is missing, stale, or uncertain.
- Use uppercase filenames `AGENTS.md` and `MEMORY.md`.
- Build MCG for general consumers with simple installation and minimal required human regression testing.
- Keep every primary interface in the first complete release: native GUI, CLI, daemon/headless mode, OpenAI-compatible provider API, and MCP client/server.
- Deeply integrate automated unit, integration, scenario, contract, and end-to-end testing.
- Run builds and automated tests locally only. Do not use GitHub Actions or any GitHub-hosted automation that can incur usage charges.
- Use the owner-controlled Ubuntu VM at `192.168.50.109` for Linux desktop, daemon, CLI, and test qualification; never persist its credentials in repository files or memory.
- Use the owner-controlled `Win11-VM` through Chrome Remote Desktop for clean Windows consumer installation and UAT; treat its browser/authentication state as stale until freshly verified.
- Do not wait for owner permission before routine work on the disposable qualification VMs. Pause only when the VM action itself is running or when a genuinely non-automatable external interaction blocks progress; execute in-scope VM administration and qualification directly while the owner is away.
- Production completion requires the agent to perform real user acceptance testing on Windows, Linux, and macOS through computer-use or equivalent user-facing interaction, fix material defects found, and leave minimal routine human regression work.
- Keep the full PRD vision in scope; use multiple real projects, initially websites/apps, to refine the system.
- Support OpenAI OAuth and API keys, OpenRouter, local OpenAI-compatible servers, and arbitrary OpenAI-compatible providers with user-supplied credentials.
- Discover provider models/capabilities from endpoints and probes, display known costs, and represent unknown costs honestly.
- Shadow model execution is opt-in for consumers and enabled during development/testing.
- Keep the repository private. Ship and qualify Windows, Linux, and macOS packages together now that an owner-controlled macOS VM is available.
- Default to meaningful autonomy with configurable presets and advanced capability controls.
- Do not include built-in remote harness access initially.
- Use a simple-by-default UI that blends consumer chat, coding workspace, and operations views, with an advanced mode inspired by LM Studio.
- OpenAI subscription OAuth is optional but encouraged; API-key access is equally supported when explicitly configured. Never silently switch an OAuth connection to API billing.

## Source Of Truth

When sources conflict, use this order:

1. Current files in the workspace.
2. Latest command output or freshly verified tool output.
3. This `MEMORY.md` file.
4. Chat history.

## Freshness Rules

- Treat browser state, email state, authentication state, and external service results as stale unless verified in the current session.
- Treat documented repo structure, user preferences, and decisions as durable unless current files or fresh verification contradict them.
- Use `Last verified YYYY-MM-DD` for facts that can drift.

## Privacy Rules

- Do not store secrets, tokens, passwords, private keys, or sensitive personal data.
- Store only the minimum information needed for future handoff, especially when external systems or personal data are involved.

## Setup And Commands

- Prerequisites (last verified 2026-08-14): Rust 1.93+, Node.js 24+, and npm 10+.
- Install frontend dependencies: `npm install`; use `npm ci` for clean local verification.
- Complete Windows verification: `.\scripts\verify.ps1`; complete Linux verification: `./scripts/verify.sh`.
- Rust checks: `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace`.
- Frontend checks: `npm run check`, `npm run test`, `npm run build`, `npm run test:e2e`.
- Documentation check: `npm run check:docs`.
- Run daemon: `cargo run -p mcg-daemon`; query it with `cargo run -p mcg-cli -- status`.
- Run UI development server: `npm run dev`.

## Project Map

- Project purpose: Build MCG (MetaCognitive Governor), a GUI-first, daemon-centered AI harness that can also serve as an OpenAI-compatible provider and MCP client/server.
- Main workflow: A persistent control plane routes user work through an Executive and model-agnostic agents into a typed, policy-governed tool execution plane while MCG observes and improves organization performance.
- External systems: Planned support includes OpenAI/Codex, OpenRouter, custom OpenAI-compatible model endpoints, MCP, host OS tools, Git, browsers/computer use, and SSH.
- Important paths:
  - `AGENTS.md`: repository-local agent workflow and memory rules.
  - `MEMORY.md`: durable context store.
  - `MCG_PRD.md`: current product requirements and phased vision.
  - `docs/architecture/system.md`: implementation architecture and boundaries.
  - `docs/roadmap/milestones.md`: full dependency-ordered delivery plan.
  - `docs/testing/strategy.md`: automated evidence and release test strategy.
  - `Cargo.toml`: Rust workspace and shared dependency/lint policy.
  - `apps/mcg-daemon`: persistent local runtime process.
  - `apps/mcg-cli`: `mcg` terminal client.
  - `apps/desktop`: React/Tauri desktop application.
  - `crates/`: domain, events, config, policy, providers, tools, storage, runtime, and API boundaries.

## Architecture Notes

- Proposed stack in the PRD: Rust/Tokio/Serde/SQLite daemon, Tauri desktop UI, typed tool broker, event bus, filesystem artifact storage, and optional WASM/WASI sandboxed plugins.
- Architectural invariants: owner constitution outranks MCG; agents never bypass the execution plane; meaningful self-modification is versioned, measurable, and reversible; objective evaluation is preferred over self-reported success.
- Workspace dependency direction: stable domain/config/events at the center; policy/providers/tools/storage implement boundaries; runtime orchestrates; API and GUI/CLI are adapters.
- Initial daemon API binds to `127.0.0.1:7777`; built-in remote harness access is out of initial scope.
- Provider auth modes are explicit and billing-aware: browser OAuth, device-code OAuth, API key, bearer token, or none. OAuth failure never mutates a connection into API-key billing.
- The M1 event log is authoritative; task projections are written in the same SQLite transaction and rebuilt by validated replay at daemon startup.
- The local API rejects non-loopback binds. All routes except health require a generated persistent bearer token; SSE reconnects can replay from `after` or `Last-Event-ID`.
- M3 implements typed filesystem, process/shell, HTTP/download, system, Git/worktree/patch, and memory primitives behind one policy broker. SQLite durably stores approval state, execution leases, immutable tool audit, artifact registrations, and associated task events.
- M4 uses a separate `mcg-projects` boundary for stable project snapshots, hierarchical instruction resolution, context assembly, watched-input invalidation, world-model facts, and structured memory claims. Semantic indexes are optional derivatives, never canonical memory.
- M5 uses a separate `mcg-agents` boundary for versioned organization genomes, roles/instances, work plans, agent leases, success conditions, evaluation evidence, confidence/calibration, and organizational-cost metrics.
- M9 migration 20 stores immutable supervision observations, candidate experiments, evaluation-only shadow receipts, paired benchmarks, and decisions. Candidate recording never changes the active organization head; promotion and reversion move that head only in the same transaction as the immutable decision and experiment projection. Promotion requires the declared benchmark count, confidence floor, regression ceiling, and prospective analysis; shadow receipts contain no authoritative task settlement field.
- The M6 provider proxy is authenticated by the installation bearer token and never executes externally supplied caller tools. `/v1/responses` and `/v1/chat/completions` share exact provider routing/fallback rules; authentication errors cannot cross provider or billing boundaries. `mcg:cheap` requires known cost and `mcg:local` accepts only loopback/private endpoints.
- The M6 MCP server uses one stateless `POST /mcp`; it requires per-request protocol/capability metadata and matching `MCP-Protocol-Version`, `Mcp-Method`, and tool `Mcp-Name` headers. Present Origins must be literal loopback/localhost and are rejected before authentication otherwise. Read/query tools return private-cache structured evidence; the agent-run mutation is explicitly marked destructive/open-world and remains policy-governed.
- The `mcg-mcp` client boundary stores only secret-free connection metadata/catalogs in migration 9 and bearer secrets under a separate OS credential-store service. Current HTTP configuration performs `server/discover` and `tools/list` before committing; public plaintext endpoints and URL-embedded credentials are rejected. Explicit remote agent tools use canonical `mcp::connection::tool` requests, per-lease aliases, immutable internal bindings, conservative catalog annotations, and the existing policy/approval/audit/execution-lease broker; the executor revalidates enabled catalog identity/classification before every call.
- Stdio MCP operations spawn a direct child with a minimal environment, bounded line/request sizes, deterministic termination, a five-second current discovery probe, and same-process `2025-11-25` initialize fallback. Stdio accepts no bearer credential; CLI arguments are a direct vector and advanced desktop arguments are one per line.
- Migration 8 atomically reserves interface-owned runs under an immutable request key/SHA-256 input hash. `mcg_run_agent` requires explicit Coder/Tool Worker tool lists, executes through the existing provider/policy broker, never treats a model response as objective completion, and returns existing success/non-success evidence rather than duplicating work on retry.
- Migration 11 stores one consumer evaluation checkpoint per task/run. Its state, next condition, attempt, exact approval request, batch delegation flag, evidence, failures, and unsupported conditions are transactionally coordinated with task/run/approval events and removed only by terminal evaluation settlement.
- M5 SQLite migration 5 makes organization revisions, plans, usage, and evaluations immutable evidence while runs, instances, and leases are event-backed current projections. Startup never resumes stale authority: interrupted runs become `recovering` and active instances/leases expire before reassignment.
- Provider credentials are runtime-only redacting values and are not serializable; persisted provider connections contain an explicit auth mode but no secret. OpenAI-compatible requests omit absent optional fields, and buffered calls tolerate providers such as GPU45 that always return a terminal Responses SSE stream.
- OpenAI browser OAuth uses loopback PKCE; device OAuth uses the verification-code flow; both are standalone from Codex, support refresh-token rotation, and route subscription inference only through `https://chatgpt.com/backend-api/codex`. API-key billing is a separate explicit connection and never an OAuth fallback.
- M3 policy evaluates canonical resources, not prompt claims. Invalid or weakened owner constitutions fail closed on every broker request; audit and secret-isolation requirements cannot be disabled. Denied or approval-pending requests are audited before return and never reach a host executor.
- Linux browser packages use root-owned `/opt/metacognitive-governor/browser/chrome` plus `packaging/linux/apparmor.d/metacognitive-governor-browser` to selectively permit the Chromium user-namespace sandbox on restricted Ubuntu. Never use `--no-sandbox` or disable Ubuntu's global AppArmor user-namespace restriction.

## Decisions

### 2026-07-12 - Reset Stale Project Context

- Removed old project identity, history, setup details, external-system references, and follow-ups from this memory file.
- Preserved the memory-system rules, privacy guidance, source-of-truth ordering, freshness rules, and handoff structure.

### 2026-08-13 - Product And Engineering Baseline

- Context: The owner answered initial discovery questions and asked the agent to infer sensible defaults for the rest.
- Decision: Adopt `docs/decisions/0001-product-engineering-baseline.md` as the working product/architecture baseline.
- Files: `README.md`, `MCG_PRD.md`, `docs/spec/`, `docs/decisions/0001-product-engineering-baseline.md`, `scripts/split-prd.ps1`.
- Result: Consumer audience, full interface set, provider strategy, security defaults, runtime boundaries, memory behavior, testing layers, and delivery sequence are now explicit.
- Follow-up: Resolve the short list of owner-level choices covering license/visibility, platform release order, OAuth risk posture, remote/multi-user scope, local provider connection, and visual direction.

### 2026-08-14 - Implementation Foundation

- Context: The owner authorized moving from product discovery into architecture, milestones, testing, and scaffolding.
- Decision: Use the crate/process boundaries in `docs/architecture/system.md` and delivery order in `docs/roadmap/milestones.md`.
- Files: `Cargo.toml`, `apps/`, `crates/`, `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `package.json`, `docs/architecture/`, `docs/roadmap/`, `docs/testing/`.
- Commands/tests: Rust check/fmt/clippy/test; npm documentation/typecheck/unit/build/E2E; live daemon/CLI smoke.
- Result: M0 foundation is compilable, tested, and ready for durable M1 behavior.
- Follow-up: Build M1 runtime spine without collapsing established interface boundaries.

### 2026-08-14 - Deterministic Frontend Test Runtime

- Context: GitHub Actions run `31771792457` failed on Ubuntu because root-hoisted Vitest could not resolve workspace-nested jsdom; the same test passed on Windows due to a differently resolved optional dependency.
- Decision: Declare jsdom at the npm workspace root and rely on the lockfile rather than Vitest's optional peer installation.
- Files: `package.json`, `apps/desktop/package.json`, `package-lock.json`, `.github/workflows/ci.yml`.
- Commands/tests: Clean `npm ci`, `npm run test`, and `npm run test:e2e` passed locally.
- Result: Frontend unit-test environment installation is deterministic across supported CI platforms.
- Follow-up: Begin M1 durable runtime spine implementation.

### 2026-08-14 - M1 Durable Runtime Spine

- Context: The owner authorized the complete M1 task/event, SQLite, recovery, local authentication, streaming, and restart-test slice and prohibited GitHub-hosted builds or paid automation.
- Decision: Use the append-only event log as authority, atomically maintain rebuildable task projections, require persistent local bearer authentication outside the public health route, and support durable SSE catch-up by sequence.
- Files: `crates/mcg-domain`, `crates/mcg-events`, `crates/mcg-storage`, `crates/mcg-runtime`, `crates/mcg-api`, `apps/mcg-daemon`, `apps/mcg-cli`, `docs/architecture/runtime-spine.md`, `scripts/verify.ps1`, `scripts/verify.sh`.
- Commands/tests: Both local platform scripts passed; native Windows and Linux desktop binaries built; real daemon/CLI forced-restart smoke tests passed on both platforms.
- Result: The requested M1 runtime spine is complete and cross-platform qualified without GitHub automation.
- Follow-up: Begin M2 provider fabric while retaining M1 invariants and local-only verification.

### 2026-08-14 - Production Completion Goal

- Context: The owner explicitly established the end gate for the full project.
- Decision: Keep the goal active until the complete PRD and all interfaces are production-ready on Windows and Linux, comprehensive tests pass on owner-controlled machines, installers and lifecycle behavior are qualified, and the agent completes real UAT on both platforms using computer-use or equivalent methods.
- Files: `MEMORY.md`; active Codex goal for this task.
- Result: Passing builds or automated tests alone cannot complete the goal; material defects, missing interfaces, unverified release gates, or substantial remaining human regression work keep it active.
- Follow-up: Continue with M2 provider fabric, then progress through the dependency-ordered roadmap and final cross-platform UAT.

### 2026-08-14 - M2 Provider Contract Foundation

- Context: M2 requires one canonical interface across OpenAI, OpenRouter, GPU45, and arbitrary OpenAI-compatible endpoints without obscuring authentication or cost provenance.
- Decision: Version provider contracts independently; persist secret-free connection metadata; keep credentials redacted and runtime-only; normalize Responses JSON/SSE; retry only pre-stream transient failures; omit absent fields on the wire; represent unreported prices as unknown.
- Files: `crates/mcg-providers/src/contracts.rs`, `crates/mcg-providers/src/compatible.rs`, `crates/mcg-providers/tests/gpu45_live.rs`, `docs/integrations/gpu45.md`.
- Commands/tests: `cargo test -p mcg-providers`; `cargo clippy -p mcg-providers --all-targets -- -D warnings`; `MCG_LIVE_GPU45=1 cargo test -p mcg-providers --test gpu45_live`.
- Result: Deterministic provider tests and live GPU45 discovery/inference pass. GPU45 currently returns SSE even for `stream: false`, which the adapter handles as an explicit conformance exception.
- Follow-up: Complete the remaining M2 routing and authentication layers.

### 2026-08-14 - M2 Provider Fabric Complete

- Context: The full M2 exit gate requires common provider behavior, explicit billing, standalone OAuth, dynamic discovery/probes, routing/cost behavior, and opted-in live evidence.
- Decision: Use separate browser/device OAuth and API-key connection modes; route OAuth only to the ChatGPT Codex subscription backend; never fall back on auth failure; pin each request to one deterministic route with bounded transient fallbacks; enable shadow policy by debug/test default and release opt-in.
- Files: `crates/mcg-providers/`, `docs/architecture/provider-fabric.md`, `docs/integrations/gpu45.md`, `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md`.
- Commands/tests: `scripts/verify.ps1`; Ubuntu `scripts/verify.sh`; `MCG_LIVE_GPU45=1 cargo test -p mcg-providers --test gpu45_live` on both Windows and Ubuntu.
- Result: Nineteen provider unit/fixture tests and the common fake/fixture/live conformance paths pass. Full local platform gates pass, including native desktop builds and E2E. No GitHub automation ran.
- Follow-up: Begin M3. Add OS credential-store persistence when provider connection management is wired into runtime/UI, and include the full upstream Apache license text in distributable packaging.

### 2026-08-14 - M3 Control-Plane Foundation

- Context: M3 needs all host effects to pass through typed, policy-governed requests with durable evidence and no prompt-trust boundary.
- Decision: Version the complete primitive/approval/lease/artifact/audit contract first; evaluate resolved resource capabilities against a revalidated owner constitution; audit before invoking an executor; represent credentials as references resolved only into non-serializable executor values.
- Files: `crates/mcg-domain/src/lib.rs`, `crates/mcg-policy/src/lib.rs`, `crates/mcg-tools/src/{contracts,broker,secrets,evidence}.rs`.
- Commands/tests: `cargo test -p mcg-policy -p mcg-tools`; `cargo clippy -p mcg-policy -p mcg-tools --all-targets -- -D warnings`; `cargo fmt --all -- --check`; `git diff --check`.
- Result: Fourteen focused tests pass. Denied and approval-pending fixture requests produce audit records and zero executor calls; artifacts are SHA-256 addressed; secret debug/output is redacted; duplicate active leases are rejected. A later same-day checkpoint integrated these controls into broker execution and completed the filesystem primitive family through capability handles.
- Follow-up: Implement the remaining host executors, then persist approvals/audits and expose daemon API/CLI lifecycle operations.

### 2026-08-14 - M3 Complete Execution And Evidence Plane

- Context: M3 requires every supported host effect to use typed contracts, constitutional capability checks, durable approvals/evidence, and externally observable verification.
- Decision: Keep host execution exhaustive behind `HostToolExecutor`; use exact executable/shell capability entries, project-confined filesystem/Git paths, credential references, bounded/redacted output, no automatic HTTP redirects, and atomic SQLite approval/audit/event transactions. Do not expose raw execution over transport until project orchestration can construct safe scoped capabilities.
- Files: `crates/mcg-tools/`, `crates/mcg-policy/`, `crates/mcg-storage/`, `crates/mcg-runtime/`, `crates/mcg-api/`, `apps/mcg-cli/`, `docs/architecture/tool-policy-plane.md`, `scripts/verify.ps1`.
